# ros-build-farm-statuses

MRPT (and related libs) ROS build farm dashboard.

Binary build ("Build releases" / "BinBuild") cells stack the badges for that ROS 2 distro, in this order (when available):

- Ubuntu (`amd64`)
- Ubuntu (`arm64`)
- RHEL (`x86_64`): RHEL 8 for Humble, RHEL 9 for Jazzy and Kilted, RHEL 10 for Lyrical and Rolling.
- Fedora (`x86_64`): only since ROS 2 Lyrical.

A missing badge means that platform is not offered by the build farm for that package and distro.
